        If Black moves `f3h4`, the pawn at g3 captures.
        If Black moves `f3d2`, the queen at d1 or pawn at e2/c2 captures.
        If Black moves `f3e1`, the rook at f1 captures.
        If Black moves `f3g1`, the rook at f1 captures.
        If Black moves `f3h2`, the king at h1 captures.

        So `d8d3` loses the queen.

    *   This is tough. The knight at f3 is trapped.
        If the knight is trapped and cannot be defended, the professional move is to get something in return for it or to improve the rest of the position.
